# Playability: playtester-parent, 2026-10-22

Scored by the `playtester-parent` profile under the `playtest` skill (`.claude/skills/playtest/SKILL.md`) from each game's play pack, and written into `src/config/catalogue.json` by `scripts/playability-apply.mjs` (#2206): the parent's score is each game's own `games.<id>.playability`; a game scored in more than one year takes its lowest.

| Game | Year | Score | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| r-count | reception | 9 | Counting objects with a friendly voice, just what a Reception child should be doing. |
| r-numeral | reception | 7 | Good numeral-to-quantity practice, but the voice never says the number and the dots are small to count in a moving bubble. |
| r-subitise | reception | 7 | Clear and kind counting to five, but the dots never vanish, so it is counting rather than quick recognition. |
| r-compare | reception | 6 | Right idea for Reception, but two answers make it a guess, and the spoken question takes much of the flight. |
| r-onemore | reception | 8 | One more and one less within 10 with a ten frame, right for Reception, though a wrong answer is not explained. |
| r-measure | reception | 6 | Says it compares lengths, but same-size pictures and two answers make it general knowledge and a guess. |
| r-bonds | reception | 9 | Number bonds to 5 on a ten frame, exactly what Reception practises, with a picture that shows the answer. |
| r-add | reception | 9 | Adding with pictures that suit a Reception child, well spoken and gently marked, though the voice eats into the time to slice. |
| r-sub | reception | 9 | Take-away within five with objects to count, and a mistake shows the picture of why. |
| r-counton | reception | 6 | Sound counting practice, but bare numerals with no picture, and the spoken sequence leaves too little time to slice. |
| r-order | reception | 8 | Clear spoken ordering within 10 for Reception; calm and fair, if rather the same task five times. |
| r-patterns | reception | 8 | Copying and continuing simple repeating patterns, clearly spoken; a calm game a parent would happily allow again. |
| r-balance | reception | 4 | Really a counting task dressed as balancing, and the long spoken instruction leaves too little time to slice. |
| r-doubles | reception | 9 | Doubles shown as two coloured halves of a ten frame, a clear and fair Reception task. |
| r-shapes | reception | 6 | Names flat shapes, but the wrong choices are too different to make a child think. |
| r-share | reception | 5 | Right idea for Reception, but the long spoken question leaves under two seconds to answer and no picture of the sharing. |
| r-oddeven | reception | 5 | Odd and even is the right idea, but a yes-no guess with no pairing shown teaches little. |
| r-rhyme | reception | 6 | Rhyme spotting is worthwhile, but two pictures a wave makes it half guesswork. |
| r-sounds | reception | 6 | Matches Reception letter-sound work, but four letters to scan before the bubbles fall is too much in the time. |
| r-syllables | reception | 6 | Clapping beats is useful early on, but with only two choices a child can guess half the answers. |
| r-soundhunt | reception | 6 | Good phonics task, but the bubbles have nearly gone by the time the voice finishes. |
| r-storytime | reception | 8 | Listening comprehension at a calm pace: the voice finishes before any bubble rises. |
| r-capitals | reception | 5 | Useful letter matching for Reception, but the wrong letters are too easy and the voice leaves little time to slice. |
| r-tricky | reception | 6 | The right tricky words for Reception, but too little time to read three words after hearing the target. |
| r-build | reception | 8 | Real phonics spelling with a spoken word and gentle lives, though a child may need showing once that the letters go in order. |
| r-read | reception | 8 | Real blending practice with sensible picture choices; a parent would see their child reading. |
| r-sentence | reception | 7 | Gentle, spoken sentence building for Reception, though the sentence is printed on the card so the odd word out is easy to spot. |
| r-storyorder | reception | 9 | A calm, spoken story-sequencing game a Reception child can play alone and learn from; only the bubbles could be bigger. |
| r-trace | reception | 7 | Gentle and unhurried, but tracing that only checks coverage does not teach how to form the letter. |
| r-initial | reception | 4 | Initial sounds are the right target, but pictures with more than one name and a tight flight make it unfair for Reception. |
| y1-bonds | year1 | 9 | Number bonds with a ten-frame that shows the answer after a slip; I would let her play it again tomorrow. |
| y1-bonds-10 | year1 | 7 | Calm, kind bonds-to-10 practice with no hearts to lose, but it stays at the same easy level. |
| y1-bonds-20 | year1 | 7 | Right range for Year 1 bonds to 20 and gentle on mistakes, but a slip only shows the answer, not why. |
| y1-add | year1 | 7 | Sound addition practice for Year 1, though the later stages fly faster than a six-year-old can comfortably answer. |
| y1-story | year1 | 4 | Lovely picture-supported word problems for Year 1, but the answers have nearly gone by the time the story has been read aloud. |
| y1-sub | year1 | 8 | Clear, quick Year 1 subtraction my child can start alone, though the last stages fly faster than a 5-year-old can slice. |
| y1-missing | year1 | 8 | Proper Year 1 missing-number practice a child can start alone; later stages will move quickly. |
| y1-doubles | year1 | 9 | Doubles with a picture that shows why, short stages and a cheer; a good use of screen time. |
| y1-doubles-halves | year1 | 8 | Sound halving and doubling practice with a calm no-lives mode, though the bubbles are a little small and quick for a five-year-old. |
| y1-skip | year1 | 8 | Clear counting-in-twos practice a Year 1 child can start alone; later stages will be quick. |
| y1-moreless | year1 | 6 | Exactly the Year 1 one more, one less skill, but the bubbles leave the screen soon after the question is spoken. |
| y1-words | year1 | 6 | Clear Year 1 number-word reading, but the wrong answers are too easy to spot to stretch my child. |
| y1-half | year1 | 8 | Halves of small quantities with cookie pictures, matching Year 1, but the picture is not used to show the halving after a mistake. |
| y1-arrays | year1 | 6 | A good pictorial start on multiplication, but counting the array takes longer than the bubbles allow. |
| y1-share | year1 | 6 | Right Year 1 sharing with pictures, but the spoken question uses up the whole flight and the cookies are never shown being shared. |
| y1-coins | year1 | 6 | Pleasant, but the value is printed on each coin, so it only looks like coin recognition. |
| y1-time | year1 | 6 | Spot-on Year 1 o'clock practice with clever distractors, but the small time labels fall before my child can read them. |
| y1-timewords | year1 | 6 | Easy to start and gentle, but rabbit-or-tortoise choices teach a Year 1 child very little. |
| y1-order | year1 | 7 | Easy to start and fair, but stage 1 orders single digits a Year 1 child already knows. |
| y1-compare | year1 | 7 | Clear and kind on mistakes, but the first stage compares groups that are too far apart to make a Year 1 child think. |
| y1-line | year1 | 8 | A clear number-line game a Year 1 child can start alone; stage 1 is gentle and the later stages will get fast. |
| y1-shapes | year1 | 6 | Names the right 2-D shapes but the wrong answers are too easy to rule out to teach much. |
| y1-shapes3d | year1 | 6 | Everyday objects for 3-D shapes is a nice idea, but showing a pyramid as a flat triangle teaches the wrong thing. |
| y1-position | year1 | 5 | Names directions, which Year 1 needs, but stage 1 is mostly spotting the up arrow and the word answers come too fast. |
| y1-length | year1 | 2 | Looks like measuring but is really a two-way colour guess, with the colour words on the wrong-coloured bubbles and no pictures of the things compared. |
| y1-mass | year1 | 2 | Heavy and light becomes a two-way colour guess about gram numbers, with no picture of what is being weighed. |
| y1-capacity | year1 | 2 | Confusing: the colour names do not match the bubble colours, no bottles are drawn and the spoken question outlasts the wave. |
| y1-months | year1 | 6 | The right calendar facts for Year 1, but the day-name bubbles fly past before a beginning reader can read them. |
| y1-when | year1 | 3 | The right Year 1 time words, but a confusing picture clue and bubbles that fall as the voice finishes make it frustrating. |
| y1-balance | year1 | 7 | Teaches what the equals sign means at a fair pace in stage 1, though the later stages hurry. |
| y1-contractions | year1 | 3 | Year 2 content with a long instruction and two-word answers that a Year 1 child cannot read in time. |
| y1-digraphs | year1 | 6 | Exactly the digraphs Year 1 is taught, but there is not enough time to hear, think and slice. |
| y1-alien | year1 | 6 | The right phonics-check practice, but a Year 1 reader cannot decode three words before the bubbles fall. |
| y1-soundhunt | year1 | 4 | Good Year 1 phonics, but the bubbles have fallen before the voice has finished saying the words, so my child cannot answer in time. |
| y1-storytime | year1 | 5 | A calm listening game that waits for the story, but the questions are too easy and the names fall before they are read. |
| y1-spelling | year1 | 4 | Right tricky words for Year 1, but the duplicated card, random wrong letters and fast bubbles make it more guessing than spelling. |
| y1-spellrules | year1 | 6 | Real Year 1 spelling-rule practice with believable misspellings, but my child cannot read three words before they fall. |
| y1-spellrules2 | year1 | 6 | Teaches the oi/oy spelling choice well, but the bubbles fall faster than a Year 1 child reads three words. |
| y1-plurals | year1 | 6 | The right Year 1 spelling rule, but there is far too little time to hear the question and read three endings. |
| y1-compound | year1 | 6 | Good spelling practice with real slips as distractors, but reading three long words in five seconds is beyond a Year 1 reader. |
| y1-suffix | year1 | 4 | The right Year 1 endings, but a sentence that fits two answers and four word bubbles that fall too fast make it unfair. |
| y1-prefix | year1 | 6 | Good un- prefix practice, but three words on moving bubbles is too much reading in the time for a Year 1 child. |
| y1-punct | year1 | 6 | The capital-letter rule is spot on for Year 1, but the word bubbles leave too little time to read and choose. |
| y1-alphabet | year1 | 6 | Useful alphabet-order practice, but switching between before and after leaves too little time to think. |
| y1-days | year1 | 5 | Right words for Year 1, but most wrong letters are giveaways and the bubbles move too fast. |
| y1-syllables | year1 | 6 | A good clapping-syllables idea with pictures, but my child needs more time to clap the word before the bubbles fall. |
| y1-sentence | year1 | 4 | Good Year 1 sentences, but the last words fly past too fast to catch, so the child loses hearts to speed, not reading. |
| y1-storyorder | year1 | 7 | Calm and easy to follow, but the stories are told in order and match Reception's, so a Year 1 child learns little. |
| y1-trace | year1 | 8 | Calm and easy to start, but it checks that the letter is covered, not that it is formed the right way, which is what Year 1 handwriting needs. |
| y1-tracenum | year1 | 8 | Gentle and clear, but a scribble that covers the digit seems to pass, so it does not teach correct number formation. |
| y2-pv | year2 | 7 | Quick, clear place-value practice at the right level, only short of an explanation and variety. |
| y2-compare | year2 | 7 | Practises the Year 2 signs with the right trap of < against >, but every wave looks the same and later stages speed up past a child's pace. |
| y2-skip | year2 | 8 | Proper Year 2 counting in steps with fair wrong answers; the later stages fly too fast for a careful counter. |
| y2-add | year2 | 6 | Adding within 100 is right for Year 2, but the stage-one sums are too easy, the wrong answers are not real slips, and later stages fly faster than a child can work. |
| y2-sub | year2 | 8 | Proper Year 2 subtraction that rises to crossing ten; the later stages will rush children still counting back. |
| y2-related | year2 | 8 | The best of the batch: proper Year 2 related facts with the helping fact on screen, though later stages are quick. |
| y2-three | year2 | 7 | Right Year 2 skill at a fair first pace, though later stages outrun the working time and mistakes show only the answer. |
| y2-inverse | year2 | 8 | Solid Year 2 missing-number practice that a child can start alone; later stages will move faster than the working needs. |
| y2-story-add | year2 | 4 | Good Year 2 word problems, but the bubbles have gone before a seven-year-old has read the question. |
| y2-anyorder | year2 | 4 | A useful idea for Year 2, but a yes-or-no guess with a wrapped sum and too little time to read it teaches little. |
| y2-tables | year2 | 7 | Solid Year 2 times-tables practice with sensible wrong answers; the last stages get tight and a mistake shows only the answer. |
| y2-tables-2 | year2 | 6 | Calm, no-lives practice of one Year 2 table; useful drill, but flat and samey across the round. |
| y2-tables-5 | year2 | 6 | Calm, no-lives practice of one Year 2 table; useful drill, but flat and samey across the round. |
| y2-tables-10 | year2 | 6 | Calm, no-lives practice of one Year 2 table; useful drill, but flat and samey across the round. |
| y2-story-times | year2 | 5 | Real Year 2 multiplication with a helpful array, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles fly. |
| y2-oddeven | year2 | 6 | Right topic for Year 2, but a fifty-fifty guess each time and later stages are too quick. |
| y2-fractions | year2 | 8 | Clear pictures, the Year 2 fractions and real slips like 4/1 as wrong answers; only the faster later stages will trip a child up. |
| y2-equiv | year2 | 5 | The circle pictures are clear and match Year 2's half equals two quarters, but there is little else to practise and the question line is long for the time given. |
| y2-money | year2 | 7 | Real coins and real Year 2 totals, though the adding barely fits the time even at stage 1. |
| y2-story-money | year2 | 4 | The right money problems for Year 2, made unplayable by long sentences on a fast wave. |
| y2-coincombo | year2 | 6 | The right money skill for Year 2, but coins appear as text, not coins, and four sums to check is too much for the time the bubbles stay up. |
| y2-time | year2 | 6 | Lovely clocks and the right Year 2 times, but reading "quarter past" on four moving bubbles needs twice the time they give. |
| y2-words | year2 | 6 | Clear and quick, but stage 1 is Year 1 teen words and a mistake gets the answer without the reason. |
| y2-order | year2 | 8 | Comfortable and fair to play, but stage 1 orders numbers to 20 that a Year 2 child already knows. |
| y2-line | year2 | 6 | Clear and calm, but counting on in ones is Year 1 work and the later stages fly too fast. |
| y2-estimate | year2 | 7 | Number-line estimation fits Year 2 and is quick to grasp, but the voice talks over the whole flight and the small tick marks are hard to count at speed. |
| y2-shapes | year2 | 6 | Good Year 2 shape recognition with a smart 2-D distractor, but the bubbles fall before the question is read. |
| y2-objects3d | year2 | 6 | Good Year 2 shape names, but four word bubbles plus the question are too much to read before they fall. |
| y2-faces | year2 | 5 | The right Year 2 shape knowledge, but the 3-D pictures are tiny emoji and the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y2-symmetry | year2 | 5 | Right Year 2 topic with clear grids, but a coin-flip answer and a voice that outlasts the first wave limit its value. |
| y2-patterns | year2 | 6 | Calm and clear, but simple AB patterns with giveaway distractors are too easy for Year 2. |
| y2-position | year2 | 5 | Right Year 2 turning vocabulary, but long questions with no picture leave too little time to slice. |
| y2-length | year2 | 4 | The right Year 2 units, but the wordy question beats the bubbles and one wave asks which is longer with nothing to compare. |
| y2-mass | year2 | 4 | Right Year 2 units of mass, but the question takes longer to read than the bubbles stay, and a red-or-blue wave has nothing to weigh. |
| y2-capacity | year2 | 4 | Units of capacity suit Year 2, but a "which holds more?" question with no containers to look at leaves the child guessing. |
| y2-measure-compare | year2 | 7 | Proper Year 2 comparison with < > =, but the bubbles leave before a careful child has decided. |
| y2-temp | year2 | 4 | Looks like temperature work but is a two-number comparison, and the reading needed far outruns the bubbles. |
| y2-scales | year2 | 4 | Looks like reading a scale but is really counting on, and the question names a ribbon that is not there. |
| y2-duration | year2 | 5 | Asks the right Year 2 facts about time, but with no clock to look at and two giveaway answers each wave it is a thin quiz. |
| y2-balance | year2 | 8 | Teaches the equals sign as balance with the very mistakes children make, though later stages will be too quick for the two-step working. |
| y2-stats | year2 | 6 | Right topic for Year 2, but stage 1 asks for counts a Reception child could read off. |
| y2-stats-order | year2 | 7 | Real Year 2 tally-chart work at a comfortable pace, but a highlighted top row and a stray line across the chart blunt it. |
| y2-stats-table | year2 | 6 | A pure look-up with wrong answers that are not in the table, so a child learns little about reading data. |
| y2-storytime | year2 | 6 | Calm read-aloud comprehension, but the questions are easy retrieval with wrong answers that are not in the story. |
| y2-spelling | year2 | 6 | Uses Year 2 exception words but blanks the easy letter, so it practises phonics children already know. |
| y2-contractions | year2 | 5 | Contractions are Year 2 spelling, but the wrong choices give the answer away and the apostrophe is never explained. |
| y2-suffix | year2 | 5 | The right Year 2 suffixes, but too much to read per wave and one question where two endings are correct. |
| y2-nouns | year2 | 5 | Good Year 2 grammar, but the sentence takes far longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y2-suffix-root | year2 | 6 | Exactly the Year 2 suffix rule with realistic misspellings, but three words to read on every bubble leaves far too little time to choose. |
| y2-homophones | year2 | 6 | Exactly the Year 2 homophones, but the sentence and the look-alike words need more reading time than the bubbles allow. |
| y2-spell-kn | year2 | 7 | Sound silent-k practice with the misspelling children really make; the rule itself is never explained. |
| y2-spell-le | year2 | 7 | Useful -le spelling practice with believable mistakes, calm but repetitive within a stage. |
| y2-spell-dge | year2 | 7 | Good -dge practice with realistic misspellings, though every wave looks alike and the later stages rush the reader. |
| y2-spell-c | year2 | 6 | Right Year 2 rule, but the wrong spellings never test the c-or-s choice the game is named for. |
| y2-spell-tion | year2 | 7 | Solid -tion practice with realistic misspellings; a reason for the spelling would make the mistakes teach. |
| y2-spell-more | year2 | 6 | Fair Year 2 words, but a parent would be puzzled that 'Walk or Wawk?' asks about mother and brother. |
| y2-wordclass | year2 | 6 | Good Year 2 grammar with well-chosen sentences, but four words to read per wave leaves too little time. |
| y2-sentencetype | year2 | 4 | Right Year 2 grammar idea, but the question mark gives the answer away and the reading is too heavy for the time. |
| y2-tense | year2 | 5 | A real Year 2 grammar point, but a sentence to read on a five-second wave and time words that give the answer away. |
| y2-punct | year2 | 6 | Proper Year 2 punctuation practice, but the bubbles fall before a child has read and decided. |
| y2-sentence | year2 | 4 | The sentence is copied from the card, and its last words vanish too fast to catch, so it frustrates more than it teaches. |
| y2-conjunction | year2 | 6 | Good Year 2 grammar with real conjunction choices, but a whole sentence plus three word bubbles cannot be read before they fall. |
| y2-infer | year2 | 6 | Good Year 2 inference questions, but there is far too much to read for a child to answer before the bubbles fall. |
| y2-trace | year2 | 8 | Calm and kind, with helpful prompts, but tracing outline print is below what Year 2 handwriting needs. |
| y3-count | year3 | 8 | Varied Year 3 counting and place-value practice with honest wrong answers; the later stages will feel rushed. |
| y3-pv | year3 | 6 | Exactly the place-value check Year 3 needs, but the bubbles leave just before a typical reader has worked it out. |
| y3-compare | year3 | 7 | Quick, clean practice comparing three-digit numbers, though every round is the same sign choice and the later stages get tight. |
| y3-line | year3 | 7 | Fair Year 3 estimating on a number line to 1,000; the wrong answer is named but not shown on the line. |
| y3-words | year3 | 7 | The right Year 3 skill with clever wrong answers, but later stages give too little time to read the words. |
| y3-mental | year3 | 7 | Exactly the Year 3 mental adding statement with place-value traps; the later stages will feel rushed. |
| y3-column | year3 | 9 | Real Year 3 subtraction practice my child could start alone, though a mistake shows only the answer, never the column working. |
| y3-check | year3 | 6 | Estimating is exactly what Year 3 should practise, with real wrong answers, but rounding two numbers leaves too little time before the bubbles fall. |
| y3-missing | year3 | 7 | Good Year 3 inverse-operation practice with real wrong answers, but the faster stages leave little thinking time. |
| y3-story-as | year3 | 5 | Right-level word problems, but there is not time to read and work them out before the bubbles drop. |
| y3-tables | year3 | 9 | Strong Year 3 tables practice with doubling hints; only the narrator talking over each wave lets it down. |
| y3-tables-3 | year3 | 7 | Calm 3 times table practice with no lives to lose, but it stays flat and a wrong answer gets no hint. |
| y3-tables-4 | year3 | 8 | Calm 4 times table practice that teaches doubling, though it does not get harder as it goes. |
| y3-tables-8 | year3 | 8 | Calm 8 times table practice built on doubling the 4s, though it stays at one level throughout. |
| y3-multiply | year3 | 8 | Good place-value multiplication with a helpful hint fact, though the narrator talks over every wave and later stages get quick. |
| y3-story | year3 | 4 | Good Year 3 scaling problems, but a fifteen-word story cannot be read before the bubbles fall. |
| y3-tenths | year3 | 8 | Clear counting in tenths that suits Year 3; a wrong answer shows the right one but not the step. |
| y3-fracof | year3 | 6 | Calm and easy to start, but stage 1 is Year 2 sharing and the star picture is never split up to show why. |
| y3-fracline | year3 | 5 | Fractions on a number line is right for Year 3, but the line is drawn as broken dashes and the labels give the answer away. |
| y3-fracequiv | year3 | 7 | Clear diagrams for Year 3 equivalent fractions with believable wrong answers; it only gets tight in the later, faster stages. |
| y3-fracadd | year3 | 7 | Solid Year 3 fraction sums with the classic add-the-denominators trap, but no picture to show why when a child gets it wrong. |
| y3-fraccompare | year3 | 7 | Tests the key Year 3 idea that a bigger denominator means a smaller piece, but a wrong answer gets no picture to fix the misconception. |
| y3-measures | year3 | 4 | Calls itself measuring but shows only a small dashed line, and the reading load is too heavy for the time the bubbles stay up. |
| y3-perimeter | year3 | 6 | A sound Year 3 perimeter task with a clear picture, but counting edges takes longer than the bubbles stay. |
| y3-money | year3 | 4 | Money matters to parents, but the coins are hard to read on a phone and the wrong answers let a child skip adding the pence. |
| y3-time | year3 | 7 | Good Year 3 clock reading, but later stages leave too little time to count minutes on a face with no minute marks. |
| y3-roman | year3 | 8 | Clear, quick Roman numeral practice at the right level; a wrong answer would teach more with a short reason. |
| y3-calendar | year3 | 5 | The right Year 3 calendar facts, but the wrong answers miss the real confusions and the long questions leave little time to slice. |
| y3-angles | year3 | 5 | Good Year 3 angle and turn questions, but the angle is drawn small and the bubbles leave before a seven-year-old has read the question. |
| y3-charts | year3 | 6 | Clear, well-drawn Year 3 bar charts with honest wrong answers, but reading the question, the labels and the scale takes longer than the bubbles stay. |
| y3-wordlist | year3 | 7 | Real Year 3-4 word-list spelling from a spoken word, with smallish letters and a confusing double row of blanks. |
| y3-prefix | year3 | 6 | Real Year 3 prefix vocabulary, but reading the question and three words takes longer than the bubbles stay. |
| y3-suffix | year3 | 6 | Well-chosen Year 3 suffix spellings, but comparing three similar words is a rush at this speed. |
| y3-double | year3 | 7 | A real Year 3 spelling rule with the misspelling children actually write, though the words on the bubbles are small and the rounds are samey. |
| y3-sounds | year3 | 7 | Useful Year 3 spelling choices that start well, but later stages will feel rushed. |
| y3-homophones | year3 | 6 | Good Year 3 homophone pairs in real sentences, but reading the sentence and both spellings takes longer than the bubbles stay up. |
| y3-dictionary | year3 | 9 | A brisk, calm way to practise dictionary order by the second letter, though a mistake is not explained. |
| y3-dictation | year3 | 6 | Looks like dictation but is word ordering with ready-spelt words, so the learning is thinner than its name. |
| y3-an | year3 | 7 | A proper Year 3 grammar point with a kind rule hint, but every round is the same two-bubble choice and later stages leave too little time to read the sentence. |
| y3-wordfamily | year3 | 5 | A Year 3 word-family idea let down by giveaway wrong answers and too little time to read. |
| y3-conjunctions | year3 | 4 | Right Year 3 grammar topic, but the sentence is too long to read before the bubbles fall and the wrong words are easy to rule out. |
| y3-perfect | year3 | 6 | Right grammar for Year 3, but the sentence and choices cannot be read before the bubbles fall. |
| y3-speech | year3 | 5 | Exactly the Year 3 punctuation point, but comparing two sentences in five seconds will end in frustration. |
| y4-count | year4 | 6 | Thousands practice with sensible wrong answers, but stage 1 is easy and the 6s, 7s and 9s the title promises were not seen. |
| y4-negative | year4 | 7 | Right Year 4 negative-number practice a child can do alone, but samey and too quick at later stages. |
| y4-pv | year4 | 7 | Solid Year 4 place-value practice, but tight on time and samey within a stage. |
| y4-round | year4 | 7 | Good Year 4 rounding practice, but crowded bubbles and short flights make it a scramble. |
| y4-roman | year4 | 6 | Right Year 4 Roman numerals, but the II card looks like the pause button and later stages get quick. |
| y4-column | year4 | 9 | Proper Year 4 four-digit subtraction with time to think, but a wrong answer gets the answer only, not the working. |
| y4-mistake | year4 | 6 | Good reasoning task for Year 4, but checking three columns in five seconds will frustrate most children. |
| y4-story | year4 | 5 | Well-built Year 4 two-step problems with honest distractors, but no child can read and work them out before the bubbles fall. |
| y4-whichop | year4 | 5 | A useful reasoning skill with a good "how many more" trap, but the story is too long to read before the bubbles fall and the voice garbles it. |
| y4-tables | year4 | 9 | Brisk, fair mixed tables practice that matches the Year 4 check, held back only by the bare answer on a mistake. |
| y4-tables-6 | year4 | 7 | Calm, fair 6× table drill well within reach, but one flat round of one table with no worked help on a mistake. |
| y4-tables-7 | year4 | 7 | Calm, fair 7× table drill well within reach, but one flat round of one table with no worked help on a mistake. |
| y4-tables-9 | year4 | 7 | Calm, fair 9× table drill well within reach, but one flat round of one table with no worked help on a mistake. |
| y4-tables-11 | year4 | 7 | Calm, fair 11× table drill well within reach, but one flat round of one table with no worked help on a mistake. |
| y4-tables-12 | year4 | 7 | Calm, fair 12× table drill well within reach, but one flat round of one table with no worked help on a mistake. |
| y4-mental | year4 | 9 | Quick, clear practice of multiplying by 0 and 1 that a Year 4 child can do alone, though a mistake is corrected without the rule. |
| y4-factorpairs | year4 | 6 | Strong Year 4 tables thinking, but four products to check is too much for the time the bubbles stay up. |
| y4-shortmult | year4 | 6 | Right topic for Year 4, but giveaway distractors let a child answer from the units digit and the later stages are too quick for the working. |
| y4-story-md | year4 | 6 | Called problems but stage 1 is a pre-partitioned addition, so it looks like multiplication practice without really being it. |
| y4-fracequiv | year4 | 6 | Clear fraction bars, but the task reduces to counting parts, so the equivalence Year 4 needs is hardly practised. |
| y4-hundredths | year4 | 7 | Real Year 4 hundredths practice that a child can start alone, but the later stages leave too little time and a mistake only shows the answer. |
| y4-fracof | year4 | 6 | Sound practice, but stage 1 stays at Year 3 unit fractions and the wrong answers are random numbers. |
| y4-fracadd | year4 | 8 | Excellent Year 4 fraction addition with the add-the-denominators trap; top stages are tight. |
| y4-decimals | year4 | 8 | Quick, focused Year 4 tenths practice; the top stages leave only just enough time. |
| y4-div10 | year4 | 8 | Exactly the Year 4 divide-by-10 skill with the mistakes children really make; later stages are a little rushed. |
| y4-comparedec | year4 | 6 | Rounding decimals is the right Year 4 skill, but two of four answers are obvious giveaways and later stages are rushed. |
| y4-money | year4 | 8 | Useful, varied money practice at the right level for Year 4; later stages get tight and a slip is not explained. |
| y4-convert | year4 | 8 | Good Year 4 unit conversion with realistic wrong answers; only the later stages get too quick to work it out. |
| y4-area | year4 | 6 | Area by counting squares, as Year 4 asks, but stage 1 rectangles are easy and the later stages fly too fast to count. |
| y4-time | year4 | 6 | Clear clock with honest distractors, but stage 1 is o'clock and quarter-to reading well below the Year 4 24-hour work it names. |
| y4-shapes | year4 | 5 | A long question with no picture and a coin-flip answer, falling away before most Year 4 children have read it. |
| y4-angles | year4 | 6 | Teaches the right Year 4 angle words, but the drawing is very small and the same three answers repeat every wave. |
| y4-symmetry | year4 | 7 | Sound Year 4 symmetry with tempting distractors, but no fold is shown when a child gets it wrong and later stages are rushed. |
| y4-coords | year4 | 8 | Proper Year 4 coordinates with the swapped-order trap built in; bubbles crowd under a big card in later stages. |
| y4-graphs | year4 | 6 | Clear charts, but stage 1 only asks for single-bar reading, below what Year 4 is taught. |
| y4-retrieve | year4 | 6 | Calm and spoken aloud, but the answer is copied word for word from the text, which is too easy for Year 4. |
| y4-context | year4 | 5 | Useful vocabulary-in-context practice, but there is far too much to read before the two bubbles fall away. |
| y4-wordlist | year4 | 6 | The right Year 4 spellings, but letters come round too briefly and too small, which risks tears. |
| y4-prefix-not | year4 | 6 | Good prefix practice with a helpful hint, but there is too much to read before the bubbles fall. |
| y4-sure-ture | year4 | 6 | Right -ture/-sure spellings for Year 4, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles allow. |
| y4-ous | year4 | 8 | Good Year 4 spelling practice with believable wrong spellings; a little rushed at later stages. |
| y4-shun | year4 | 6 | Exactly the -cian/-tion spelling Year 4 learns, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay in reach. |
| y4-origins | year4 | 6 | Right Year 4 spelling pattern, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y4-proofread | year4 | 6 | Proofreading with Key Stage 1 words, and a mistake never shows the correct spelling. |
| y4-homophones | year4 | 6 | The right Year 3-4 homophones, but the sentence takes longer to read than the two bubbles stay up. |
| y4-plural-poss | year4 | 6 | Exactly the apostrophe work Year 4 needs, but the phrase must be read and thought about faster than the bubbles allow. |
| y4-standard | year4 | 7 | Spot-on Year 4 grammar, but samey was/were choices and later stages that outpace a child reading the sentence. |
| y4-nounphrase | year4 | 6 | Clear and calm, but the wrong answers give the game away, so it practises Year 2 adjectives rather than Year 4 noun phrases. |
| y4-adverbials | year4 | 6 | Right Year 4 grammar, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay up, so many misses will be timing, not understanding. |
| y4-speech | year4 | 7 | Good practice of the comma before direct speech, though the sentence has to be read faster than a Year 4 child reads at later stages. |
| y4-pronouns | year4 | 5 | Easy pronoun matching rather than the Year 4 noun-or-pronoun choice, and the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles last. |
| y4-determiners | year4 | 8 | Good Year 4 grammar practice with the adjective trap; the bubbles need reading, so later stages are tight. |
| y5-pv | year5 | 8 | Proper Year 5 place value to a million with telling distractors; crowded bubbles and later speed are the friction. |
| y5-negative | year5 | 6 | Good Year 5 negative-number work, but the worded questions leave far too little time to read and count back. |
| y5-round | year5 | 7 | The right rounding for Year 5, but by stage 3 the bubbles fall before most children have decided. |
| y5-roman | year5 | 8 | Solid Roman numeral practice at the right level, though a mistake shows the answer without saying why. |
| y5-column | year5 | 7 | Excellent distractors built from real exchange errors, but a written-method sum done in the head is too rushed on a phone. |
| y5-mental | year5 | 6 | Neat and fair, but stage 1 is place-value stepping rather than the mental adding and subtracting the title promises. |
| y5-story | year5 | 4 | Real Year 5 word problems, but adding three four-digit numbers in a few seconds is not fair practice. |
| y5-factors | year5 | 8 | Quick, fair factor-pair practice for Year 5; mistakes show the answer but not the division behind it. |
| y5-primes | year5 | 8 | Comfortable to play and tests prime recall with the right traps, though the same question every wave gets samey. |
| y5-longmult | year5 | 6 | Called long multiplication but stage 1 is Year 4 short multiplication done mentally, and later stages leave no time to work. |
| y5-mentalmd | year5 | 8 | Real Year 5 mental multiplying and dividing from known facts, with distractors that catch place-value slips. |
| y5-shortdiv | year5 | 7 | Good short division practice, but the later stages rush a child who still works it out step by step. |
| y5-x10 | year5 | 8 | Brisk place-value practice that matches Year 5; a mistake could show the digits shifting. |
| y5-squares | year5 | 9 | Brisk square and cube practice at the right pace for Year 5; a mistake could show the multiplication behind it. |
| y5-equals | year5 | 7 | Strong equality thinking with a telling distractor, but two three-digit mental steps rarely fit before the bubbles fall. |
| y5-story-md | year5 | 6 | Good scaling questions for Year 5, but the words take longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y5-truefalse | year5 | 6 | Sound factor and multiple statements, but a two-choice answer lets a child guess through without the why. |
| y5-fraccompare | year5 | 6 | Clear and kind, but a two-bubble choice lets a child guess half the answers, and later stages fly too fast to think. |
| y5-fracequiv | year5 | 6 | Proper Year 5 equivalent fractions with a picture, but the question takes longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y5-mixed | year5 | 7 | Good Year 5 conversion practice, but the mixed numbers are typeset so 1 9/11 looks like 19/11. |
| y5-fracadd | year5 | 8 | Clean fraction practice with sensible distractors; it starts at Year 4 level and the clock tightens later. |
| y5-fracmult | year5 | 8 | Exactly the Year 5 objective with the right misconceptions as wrong answers; only the fastest stages squeeze the thinking time. |
| y5-decfrac | year5 | 8 | Good decimal–fraction practice with real misconceptions as distractors; it opens at Year 4 level and tightens later. |
| y5-rounddec | year5 | 7 | Teaches the "five or more rounds up" rule well, but later stages are too quick to read and decide. |
| y5-decimals | year5 | 8 | Targets the classic "longer decimal is bigger" error well, with a fair clock until the final stages. |
| y5-percent | year5 | 8 | A clear hundred-square that teaches what per cent means, squeezed only by the later stages’ speed. |
| y5-convert | year5 | 8 | Brisk metric conversion practice with sensible distractors; the right answer is shown but never why. |
| y5-imperial | year5 | 8 | Teaches the imperial equivalences Year 5 needs, with sensible wrong answers, though later stages rush the reading. |
| y5-area | year5 | 9 | Good area practice with perimeter as a real distractor and a grid that shows why, though later stages will feel rushed. |
| y5-volume | year5 | 7 | Calm and well explained, but stage 1 is square-counting rather than Year 5 volume. |
| y5-story-measure | year5 | 5 | The right decimal measure problems, but the sentences are too long to read before the bubbles fall. |
| y5-3d | year5 | 5 | Sound shape vocabulary, but at Year 2 level and the long questions leave too little time to read and slice. |
| y5-angles | year5 | 6 | The right Year 5 angle names, but the angle pictures are too small to judge comfortably on a phone. |
| y5-anglefacts | year5 | 8 | Clear, quick practice of Year 5 angle facts with sensible distractors; later stages tighten the clock. |
| y5-polygons | year5 | 6 | Reasoning about equal sides and angles is right for Year 5, but a yes/no choice is too easy to guess. |
| y5-transform | year5 | 5 | Good translation questions, but the big grid hides the bubbles and there is no time to count squares. |
| y5-linegraph | year5 | 5 | A tidy graph, but stage 1 is Year 4 read-off practice and the bubbles fall before a child has read the question and the graph. |
| y5-timetable | year5 | 6 | Useful timetable reading for Year 5, though the table takes longer to scan than the bubbles allow. |
| y5-factopinion | year5 | 5 | Fact and opinion is on the Year 5 reading list, but these sentences are too easy and the two-way choice invites guessing. |
| y5-wordlist | year5 | 5 | Good Year 5-6 spellings, but one needed letter was hardly ever reachable, so a careful child can lose for no reason. |
| y5-cious | year5 | 6 | Right spelling rule with a helpful clue word, but the sentence and clue take longer to read than the bubbles stay up. |
| y5-ant | year5 | 6 | Teaches the clue-word trick for -ant/-ent well, but there is far too much to read before the bubbles fall. |
| y5-able | year5 | 5 | Right spelling focus, but the made-up "eable" distractor gives the answer away and the sentence takes too long to read. |
| y5-fer | year5 | 6 | Teaches the -fer doubling rule directly, but the split gap looks odd and the same r/rr choice repeats every wave. |
| y5-silent | year5 | 5 | Easy silent-letter words for Year 5, and the bubbles move on before a child has said the word and chosen. |
| y5-verbs | year5 | 6 | The right suffix work for Year 5, but a child barely has time to try each ending in their head. |
| y5-verbprefix | year5 | 6 | Exactly the Year 5 prefix work, but the clue plus three words is too much to read before the bubbles fall. |
| y5-relative | year5 | 6 | Right Year 5 grammar, but the distractors are so unlike a relative pronoun that the answer picks itself. |
| y5-modal | year5 | 6 | Right Year 5 grammar, but the wrong answers are nouns, so a child spots the modal without thinking about verbs. |
| y5-parenthesis | year5 | 6 | Introduces brackets for parenthesis, but matching the visible bracket needs no understanding of what parenthesis does. |
| y5-commas | year5 | 5 | A worthwhile comma topic, but the question is ambiguous on screen and the long wording leaves no time to slice. |
| y5-cohesion | year5 | 6 | Uses the right Year 5 terms for adverbials, but the reading time is too tight and it labels single words rather than whole paragraphs. |
| y5-perfect | year5 | 6 | The right Year 5 grammar choice, but a twelve-word sentence cannot be read before the bubbles fall. |
| y6-pv | year6 | 7 | Exactly the place-value question on the key stage 2 test, but a wrong slice shows the answer without the column that explains it. |
| y6-round | year6 | 7 | Well-built Year 6 rounding questions with honest distractors; it needs a hint of why when a child gets one wrong. |
| y6-negative | year6 | 7 | Spot-on Year 6 temperature questions with real traps, but no number line on a mistake and too little time later. |
| y6-longmult | year6 | 7 | Well-chosen wrong answers for long multiplication, but the later stages leave no time to actually multiply. |
| y6-longdiv | year6 | 7 | Year 6 division with believable wrong answers, though the timer turns it into estimating rather than the written method. |
| y6-mental | year6 | 7 | Satisfying big-number mental maths for Year 6, but from stage 3 the bubbles go before a careful child has finished. |
| y6-factors | year6 | 9 | A proper prime-number puzzle with well-chosen traps like 1 and 33, though it never says why a number is not prime. |
| y6-order-ops | year6 | 6 | The left-to-right trap is exactly right for Year 6, but the ÷ is hard to tell from + and a mistake is not explained. |
| y6-story | year6 | 5 | A proper multi-step problem squeezed into half the time it takes, so it rewards guessing over working it out. |
| y6-simplify | year6 | 7 | Real fraction practice for Year 6 with clever wrong answers; the later stages will feel rushed. |
| y6-fracadd | year6 | 7 | Proper Year 6 fraction practice with honest distractors, but from stage 3 the bubbles fall before a child can convert and add. |
| y6-fracmult | year6 | 8 | Short, fair fraction multiplication at the right level; a child can keep up, though a wrong answer is corrected without the why. |
| y6-fracdiv | year6 | 8 | A brisk, well-pitched fraction drill a Year 6 child can keep up with; it shows the answer but never the step. |
| y6-fdp | year6 | 6 | Fraction-as-division is Year 6, but stage 1 is read straight off the rule line and most wrong answers give themselves away. |
| y6-decimals | year6 | 7 | Right three-decimal-place content, but the wordy question line eats the time to answer and a mistake is not explained. |
| y6-decmult | year6 | 7 | Year 6 decimal multiplication with genuinely tempting wrong answers, though the waves feel alike and errors are not explained. |
| y6-decdiv | year6 | 7 | A well-built remainders-as-decimals drill with the classic 29.1 trap, but every wave is the same and the why is never shown. |
| y6-ratio | year6 | 6 | Good Year 6 ratio problems, but the bubbles leave little more than half the time needed to read and reason them. |
| y6-percentof | year6 | 8 | Solid key stage 2 percentages practice that my child could start alone; a mistake shows the answer but not how to build it. |
| y6-formulae | year6 | 8 | Good Year 6 substitution practice with believable wrong answers, but the worded line costs reading time and errors are not explained. |
| y6-sequences | year6 | 6 | Stage 1 is counting on in steps, well below what Year 6 learns about sequences. |
| y6-algebra | year6 | 8 | Real Year 6 algebra with sensible wrong answers, but no worked step after a mistake and later stages fly too fast to solve. |
| y6-twounknowns | year6 | 5 | Looks like algebra but practises bonds to 20, and the bubbles are gone before the question is read and checked. |
| y6-convert | year6 | 8 | Test-ready unit conversions with honest distractors, let down only by unexplained mistakes and a fast later stage. |
| y6-areaperim | year6 | 6 | Calm and clear, but it practises Year 4 times tables rather than the Year 6 area-and-perimeter idea its title promises. |
| y6-area | year6 | 7 | Exactly the Year 6 triangle-area skill with a telling distractor, though every wave is the same triangle and a mistake is not explained. |
| y6-volume | year6 | 7 | Excellent distractors, including the square-unit trap, for Year 6 volume; later stages will be a scramble. |
| y6-nets | year6 | 4 | Promises nets but asks Year 2 face-counting questions with no picture, and the bubbles fall before the question is read. |
| y6-missingangles | year6 | 7 | Proper Year 6 angle reasoning with a helpful diagram, but the worded question and the later pace will cost lives. |
| y6-circles | year6 | 7 | Right Year 6 circle vocabulary, but only three letter choices each time and later stages leave too little time to match the diagram. |
| y6-coords | year6 | 7 | Strong four-quadrant practice with realistic swapped and sign-error answers, though each wave asks the same thing and mistakes go unexplained. |
| y6-transform | year6 | 5 | Right Year 6 coordinates work, but it is filed under reflection, the bubbles bunch under a large grid, and time runs short. |
| y6-pie | year6 | 6 | Looks like Year 6 statistics but stage 1 only asks for the biggest slice, which my child could do in Year 2. |
| y6-graphs | year6 | 7 | Real Year 6 graph reading, but a reading-and-looking task on a recall-speed timer, which will cost lives from stage 3. |
| y6-mean | year6 | 7 | Right Year 6 content with a classic sum-not-divided trap, but a wrong answer hides the numbers it came from. |
| y6-inference | year6 | 6 | Gentle reading practice that waits for the passage, but the stage 1 inferences are too obvious for Year 6. |
| y6-figurative | year6 | 6 | Calm simile-or-metaphor practice, but a two-way choice with the rule on screen is thin learning for Year 6. |
| y6-ough | year6 | 7 | Real Year 6 spelling practice with good distractors, but by stage 3 the bubbles leave about half the time needed to read the sentence and choose. |
| y6-homophones | year6 | 7 | Exactly the Year 6 spelling pairs, but the noun-or-verb rule is never explained and later stages outrun the reading. |
| y6-wordlist | year6 | 7 | Sound Year 6 word-list spelling with time to answer, let down by smallish letters and no help after a mistake. |
| y6-spellgap | year6 | 7 | Real Year 6 spelling practice, though the missing-word sentence is only heard, never shown. |
| y6-formal | year6 | 5 | Formal vocabulary is right for Year 6, but the wrong answers are ruled out by meaning and the letter lookup leaves too little time. |
| y6-synonyms | year6 | 6 | Good vocabulary practice with a fair antonym trap, but reading the question and three words takes longer than the bubbles allow. |
| y6-passive | year6 | 6 | Right grammar for Year 6, but with only two choices a child can guess half of it, and the later stages are rushed. |
| y6-subjunctive | year6 | 5 | Year 6 grammar my child does need, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay up, and the answer was "were" every time we saw. |
| y6-cohesion | year6 | 5 | Good test-style linking-phrase questions, but reading the list and finding the letter cannot be done before the bubbles fall. |
| y6-clauses | year6 | 5 | Year 6 punctuation with a fair comma-splice trap, but the sentence takes longer to read than the bubbles stay in reach. |
| y6-lists | year6 | 5 | The right Year 6 colon-and-list point, but a whole sentence must be read each wave and the bubbles do not wait for it. |
| y6-hyphen | year6 | 5 | A good Year 6 grammar point, but each wave needs two sentences read and the bubbles give half that time. |
| y6-subjectobject | year6 | 7 | Clear Year 6 grammar practice, though stage 1 sentences are easy and later stages leave about half the reading time needed. |
| y6-consistency | year6 | 6 | Kind and clear, but is/are/am at stage 1 is far easier than Year 6 tense and agreement work. |

- physical — `metrics.json#trace.glyphPx`: 260 px digit, 23 px brush (metrics.json#trace.brushPx); no time limit observed; manageable for Year 1
- challenge — `3-tracing.webp`: mid-trace shot is one straight vertical bar through the 3, then 4-traced.webp shows 'Zap!'; digit formation (Year 1) is not checked by direction (whether the bar alone passed: not observed)
- feedback — `5-wrong.webp`: 'Keep tracing - cover the whole number', dotted 5 still there, no heart lost (metrics.json#livesAfterWrong = 3)
- again — `6-result.webp`: three stars and a cheer; capped at challenge (1): little real practice in forming digits
